I didn't feel like I was outside myself or anything.\n\nDoctor: During the episode, did you have any gaps in memory for what you did?\n\nPatient: I don't think so. I mean, I remember being there. I just... I don't know, I don't think I forgot anything.\n\nDoctor: During the episode, were you able to respond to people around you?\n\nPatient: Not really. I mean... like at that staff meeting. I got kinda worked up and... started acting out this whole fall-prevention drill. Just moving chairs around and waving my arms. Folks kept telling me to sit down, but... I couldn't seem to stop. Just... couldn't stop what I was doing.\n\nDoctor: Do you remember feeling confused or disoriented right after the episode ended?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I just kinda... stopped. I knew where I was and everything, so... no, not confused.\n\nDoctor: Right before the episode starts, do you feel an urge, pressure, or buildup that you have to let out?\n\nPatient: No... not really. I don't feel any pressure or anything like that. It just... happens, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Does this kind of episode happen in other settings too, or only in staff meetings?\n\nPatient: I don't know...
